Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the superconductors structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 34. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 324 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on superconductors structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 34. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 324. QED.
